        Idea:
Harden the Solana payment subsystem against the attack vectors found by the adversarial security review. Fix P0: git history secret audit, prompt-injection hardening in admin_chat (remove LLM-sourced wallet addresses from initiate_payment tool; wallets must come from wallet_registry keyed by mentioned Discord user), null recipient_discord_id confirmation bypass (fail-closed). Fix P1: wallet swap window between test and final payment (verify execute_payment uses stored recipient_wallet string; reject or warn on upsert_wallet during active intents), idempotency slot reuse after forced failure (warn/DM on producer_ref collision with prior failed row having different wallet), per-payment and rolling daily USD cap on admin-triggered payouts enforced in PaymentService (default $500/payment, $2000/day/guild, exceeding goes to manual_hold), admin DM on successful admin_chat payouts over threshold (default $100, differentiated from failure DMs). Fix P2: log audit for secret leakage, document RLS posture at top of sql/payments.sql. Already using Helius so RPC attacks out of scope. Two wallets already split into solana_grants and solana_payouts providers. Think carefully about wallet-swap and idempotency — they are subtle and easy to half-fix. Goal is end-to-end hardening.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 1 of 5.
        - Actionable task IDs for this batch: ['T1', 'T2', 'T3', 'T4', 'T6', 'T7', 'T11', 'T12']

    "Idempotency collision (T6): the partial unique index uq_payment_requests_active_producer_ref is WHERE status NOT IN ('failed','cancelled'). manual_hold IS covered by the index.     "Collision detection + idempotent early-return must be reordered so collision detection runs BEFORE the same-wallet early return. A different-wallet collision against an active prior row must hit Branch B, not the early return.",

    "Rolling 24h helper MUST include in-flight statuses (pending_confirmation, queued, processing, submitted, confirmed); exclude manual_hold/failed/cancelled. Residual burst-race window (~RPC latency) is documented in the helper comment; manual_hold + admin DM is the backstop.",

  "meta_commentary": "Heavy-robustness security hardening plan across admin_chat, payments, grants, and sql. The single subtlety behind the review loop's eight flags is: for capped providers in the amount_token-only branch, derived USD must be PERSISTED into record['amount_usd'], record['token_price_usd'], AND the request_payload mirrors BEFORE create_payment_request \u2014 and only in that branch. Get T8 right and everything else (rolling helper, success-DM gate, the four new pinning tests) falls into place. Get it wrong by stamping uncapped providers and you regress test_request_payment_amount_token + test_social_publish_service. Get it wrong by NOT stamping capped providers and you silently re-open FLAG-001.\n\nExecution order matters: T1/T2/T3 first (P0, independent), then T4\u2192T5 (wallet lock depends on helper), T6 (collision, independent of T4-T5), then T7\u2192T8\u2192T9\u2192T10 (caps pipeline; T8 is the load-bearing step), then T11/T12 (docs), T13 (tests depend on all code changes), T14 (validation). T1-T4, T6, T7, T11, T12 can actually run in parallel if the executor is comfortable; the listed depends_on enforces only the hard dependencies.\n\nRead before coding: (a) src/features/payments/payment_service.py:54-202 (request_payment + confirm_payment + execute_payment), (b) src/common/db_handler.py:1417-1465 + 1853-1883 (upsert_wallet + get_payment_requests_by_producer), (c) src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:193-402 (wallet receipt through handoff), (d) src/features/grants/grants_cog.py:550-601 (second upsert_wallet caller + auto-confirm), (e) src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:580-599 + 2438-2526 (initiate_payment schema + executor), (f) src/features/payments/payment_cog.py:267-333 + 503 (terminal handler + second construction site), (g) sql/payments.sql:92-199 (status enum + partial unique index + RLS enable), (h) tests/test_admin_payments.py:497-729 + 684-709 (fake db glue + existing amount_token test), (i) tests/test_social_publish_service.py:474-685, (j) main.py:157-233 (PaymentService + PaymentCog construction).\n\nDisputed flag (DEC-014 / FLAG-004): the success criterion that bundles null-recipient with cap-breach DM wiring is a wording mismatch \u2014 null-recipient intentionally only logs WARN + returns None; the calling button view surfaces UX via its existing error path. Do NOT add an admin DM for null-recipient rejection. The reviewer may narrow the criterion text in-place.\n\nCap config must be injectable via constructor kwargs with None defaults. Existing PaymentService construction sites in tests must keep working without modification. This is non-negotiable \u2014 several tests instantiate PaymentService directly with different signatures.\n\nWhen T13 adds tests that exercise the amount_token persistence path, the fake payment provider must expose get_token_price_usd() and track price_calls. The existing tests already use this convention \u2014 follow it.\n\nIf T3 or T11 surfaces a real secret leak, STOP and flag to the user before continuing. Rotation is out of scope for this plan.",

        Idea:
Harden the Solana payment subsystem against the attack vectors found by the adversarial security review. Fix P0: git history secret audit, prompt-injection hardening in admin_chat (remove LLM-sourced wallet addresses from initiate_payment tool; wallets must come from wallet_registry keyed by mentioned Discord user), null recipient_discord_id confirmation bypass (fail-closed). Fix P1: wallet swap window between test and final payment (verify execute_payment uses stored recipient_wallet string; reject or warn on upsert_wallet during active intents), idempotency slot reuse after forced failure (warn/DM on producer_ref collision with prior failed row having different wallet), per-payment and rolling daily USD cap on admin-triggered payouts enforced in PaymentService (default $500/payment, $2000/day/guild, exceeding goes to manual_hold), admin DM on successful admin_chat payouts over threshold (default $100, differentiated from failure DMs). Fix P2: log audit for secret leakage, document RLS posture at top of sql/payments.sql. Already using Helius so RPC attacks out of scope. Two wallets already split into solana_grants and solana_payouts providers. Think carefully about wallet-swap and idempotency — they are subtle and easy to half-fix. Goal is end-to-end hardening.

    "description": "Idempotency collision detection with index-aware branching in PaymentService.request_payment (src/features/payments/payment_service.py:54-61 and 167-174). (1) Confirm get_payment_requests_by_producer at db_handler.py:1853 returns all statuses; add an inline comment documenting this invariant. (2) Rework the early-return: query all_prior_rows, normalize incoming wallet string, define INDEX_EXCLUDED={'failed','cancelled'} and TERMINAL_FOR_RETURN={'confirmed','failed','manual_hold','cancelled'}. Iterate once to find collision_row (different wallet) and blocking_prior_row (any non-index-excluded row). The idempotent early-return must ONLY fire for an active, same-wallet prior row \u2014 terminal rows MUST NOT short-circuit (add inline comment explaining why). (3) Branch A \u2014 slot reuse after terminal failure (blocking_prior_row is None): build record normally but set record['status']='manual_hold' and record['last_error']='idempotency collision: prior wallet differs', call create_payment_request, then await on_cap_breach(created). (4) Branch B \u2014 prior row still holds index slot (blocking_prior_row is not None): log WARN, await on_cap_breach(blocking_prior_row), return None without attempting any insert. (5) Fallback path at 167-174 (post-insert duplicate re-read): apply the same wallet-mismatch check against the re-read row; mismatch \u2192 log WARN, fire on_cap_breach, return None; match \u2192 return canonical row.",

(5) Compute cap_breach: per-payment cap exceeded, OR daily cap exceeded (rolling = db_handler.get_rolling_24h_payout_usd(guild_id, provider_key); rolling + cap_usd > daily_cap). (6) CRITICAL: stamp record['amount_usd']=derived_usd_for_record, record['token_price_usd']=derived_price_for_record, AND mirror onto record['request_payload']['amount_usd'] and record['request_payload']['token_price_usd'] \u2014 but ONLY inside the capped+derived branch. NEVER stamp for uncapped providers or is_test=True (that would break existing tests asserting amount_usd is None). (7) If cap_breach is set: log WARN with redacted wallet, set record['status']='manual_hold', record['last_error']=cap_breach.   "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc/src/common/db_handler.py\n@@\n logger = logging.getLogger('DiscordBot')\n \n \n+class WalletUpdateBlockedError(Exception):\n+    \"\"\"Raised when a wallet change is attempted while a payment flow is still active.\"\"\"\n+\n+\n+def _redact_wallet(wallet: Optional[str]) -> str:\n+    if not wallet:\n+        return 'unknown'\n+    wallet = str(wallet)\n+    if len(wallet) <= 10:\n+        return wallet\n+    return f\"{wallet[:4]}...{wallet[-4:]}\"\n+\n+\n def to_aware_utc(dt_str: str) -> Optional[datetime]:\n@@\n         existing = self.get_wallet(guild_id, discord_user_id, chain)\n         payload: Dict[str, Any] = {\n             'guild_id': guild_id,\n             'discord_user_id': discord_user_id,\n             'chain': chain,\n             'wallet_address': address,\n         }\n         if metadata is not None:\n             payload['metadata'] = metadata\n         if existing and existing.get('wallet_address') != address:\n+            if self.has_active_payment_or_intent(guild_id, discord_user_id):\n+                logger.warning(\n+                    \"Blocked wallet update for guild %s user %s chain %s: existing=%s incoming=%s\",\n+                    guild_id,\n+                    discord_user_id,\n+                    chain,\n+                    _redact_wallet(existing.get('wallet_address')),\n+                    _redact_wallet(address),\n+                )\n+                raise WalletUpdateBlockedError(\"active payment in flight\")\n             # Verification is tied to the specific address that received the test payment.\n             payload['verified_at'] = None\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
Harden the Solana payment subsystem against the attack vectors found by the adversarial security review. Fix P0: git history secret audit, prompt-injection hardening in admin_chat (remove LLM-sourced wallet addresses from initiate_payment tool; wallets must come from wallet_registry keyed by mentioned Discord user), null recipient_discord_id confirmation bypass (fail-closed). Fix P1: wallet swap window between test and final payment (verify execute_payment uses stored recipient_wallet string; reject or warn on upsert_wallet during active intents), idempotency slot reuse after forced failure (warn/DM on producer_ref collision with prior failed row having different wallet), per-payment and rolling daily USD cap on admin-triggered payouts enforced in PaymentService (default $500/payment, $2000/day/guild, exceeding goes to manual_hold), admin DM on successful admin_chat payouts over threshold (default $100, differentiated from failure DMs). Fix P2: log audit for secret leakage, document RLS posture at top of sql/payments.sql. Already using Helius so RPC attacks out of scope. Two wallets already split into solana_grants and solana_payouts providers. Think carefully about wallet-swap and idempotency — they are subtle and easy to half-fix. Goal is end-to-end hardening.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 3 of 5.
        - Actionable task IDs for this batch: ['T9']
        - Already completed task IDs available as dependency context: ['T1', 'T11', 'T12', 'T2', 'T3', 'T4', 'T5', 'T6', 'T7', 'T8']

    "Idempotency collision (T6): the partial unique index uq_payment_requests_active_producer_ref is WHERE status NOT IN ('failed','cancelled'). manual_hold IS covered by the index.     "Collision detection + idempotent early-return must be reordered so collision detection runs BEFORE the same-wallet early return. A different-wallet collision against an active prior row must hit Branch B, not the early return.",
    "Scheduler path (claim_due_payment_requests \u2192 execute_payment) MUST remain untouched \u2014 caps run exclusively in request_payment. Do NOT double-check caps in execute_payment or downstream retries.",

  "meta_commentary": "Heavy-robustness security hardening plan across admin_chat, payments, grants, and sql. The single subtlety behind the review loop's eight flags is: for capped providers in the amount_token-only branch, derived USD must be PERSISTED into record['amount_usd'], record['token_price_usd'], AND the request_payload mirrors BEFORE create_payment_request \u2014 and only in that branch. Get T8 right and everything else (rolling helper, success-DM gate, the four new pinning tests) falls into place. Get it wrong by stamping uncapped providers and you regress test_request_payment_amount_token + test_social_publish_service. Get it wrong by NOT stamping capped providers and you silently re-open FLAG-001.\n\nExecution order matters: T1/T2/T3 first (P0, independent), then T4\u2192T5 (wallet lock depends on helper), T6 (collision, independent of T4-T5), then T7\u2192T8\u2192T9\u2192T10 (caps pipeline; T8 is the load-bearing step), then T11/T12 (docs), T13 (tests depend on all code changes), T14 (validation). {
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc/src/features/payments/payment_cog.py\n@@\n         self._pending_terminal_handoffs: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n         self._replayed_pending_handoffs = False\n \n         self.claim_batch_size = max(int(os.getenv('PAYMENT_CLAIM_LIMIT', '10')), 1)\n         self.worker_interval_seconds = max(int(os.getenv('PAYMENT_WORKER_INTERVAL_SECONDS', '30')), 1)\n+        self._admin_success_dm_threshold_usd = float(os.getenv('ADMIN_PAYMENT_SUCCESS_DM_THRESHOLD_USD', '100'))\n+        self._admin_success_dm_providers = frozenset(\n+            provider.strip().lower()\n+            for provider in os.getenv('ADMIN_PAYMENT_SUCCESS_DM_PROVIDERS', 'solana_payouts').split(',')\n+            if provider.strip()\n+        )\n         self._startup_synced = False\n@@\n     async def _handle_terminal_payment(self, payment: Dict[str, Any]):\n         await self._notify_payment_result(payment)\n+        provider_key = str(payment.get('provider') or '').strip().lower()\n+        if (\n+            payment.get('status') == 'confirmed'\n+            and not payment.get('is_test')\n+            and provider_key in self._admin_success_dm_providers\n+            and float(payment.get('amount_usd') or 0) >= self._admin_success_dm_threshold_usd\n+        ):\n+            await self._dm_admin_payment_success(payment)\n         if payment.get('status') in {'failed', 'manual_hold'}:\n             await self._dm_admin_payment_failure(payment)\n         await self._handoff_terminal_result(payment)\n+\n+    async def _dm_admin_payment_success(self, payment: Dict[str, Any]):\n+        admin_id_env = os.getenv('ADMIN_USER_ID')\n+        if not admin_id_env:\n+            logger.warning(\n+                \"[PaymentCog] ADMIN_USER_ID not set; cannot DM admin about payment %s\",\n+                payment.get('payment_id'),\n+            )\n+            return\n+        try:\n+            admin_id = int(admin_id_env)\n+        except ValueError:\n+            logger.error(\"[PaymentCog] Invalid ADMIN_USER_ID; cannot DM admin.\")\n+            return\n+\n+        try:\n+            admin_user = await self.bot.fetch_user(admin_id)\n+        except Exception as exc:\n+            logger.error(\n+                \"[PaymentCog] Failed to fetch admin user %s for payment DM: %s\",\n+                admin_id,\n+                exc,\n+            )\n+            return\n+\n+        amount_token = float(payment.get('amount_token') or 0)\n+        amount_usd = float(payment.get('amount_usd') or 0)\n+        lines = [\n+            \"✅ **Payment Completed**\",\n+            f\"- Payment ID: `{payment.get('payment_id')}`\",\n+            f\"- Producer: `{payment.get('producer')}` / `{payment.get('producer_ref')}`\",\n+            f\"- Provider: `{payment.get('provider')}`\",\n+            f\"- Type: {'test payment' if payment.get('is_test') else 'final payment'}\",\n+            f\"- Amount: {amount_token:.8f} {self._token_label(payment)}\",\n+            f\"- USD: ${amount_usd:.2f}\",\n+            f\"- Wallet: `{_redact_wallet(payment.get('recipient_wallet'))}`\",\n+        ]\n+        if payment.get('tx_signature'):\n+            lines.append(f\"- Transaction: `{payment.get('tx_signature')}`\")\n+\n+        message = \"\\n\".join(lines)\n+        if len(message) > 1900:\n+            message = message[:1900] + \"...\"\n+\n+        try:\n+            await admin_user.send(message)\n+            logger.info(\n+                \"[PaymentCog] DM'd admin about confirmed payment %s\",\n+                payment.get('payment_id'),\n+            )\n+        except discord.Forbidden:\n+            logger.error(\"[PaymentCog] Bot forbidden from DMing admin about payment success.\")\n+        except Exception as exc:\n+            logger.error(\n+                \"[PaymentCog] Failed to DM admin about payment %s: %s\",\n+                payment.get('payment_id'),\n+                exc,\n+            )\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
Harden the Solana payment subsystem against the attack vectors found by the adversarial security review. Fix P0: git history secret audit, prompt-injection hardening in admin_chat (remove LLM-sourced wallet addresses from initiate_payment tool; wallets must come from wallet_registry keyed by mentioned Discord user), null recipient_discord_id confirmation bypass (fail-closed). Fix P1: wallet swap window between test and final payment (verify execute_payment uses stored recipient_wallet string; reject or warn on upsert_wallet during active intents), idempotency slot reuse after forced failure (warn/DM on producer_ref collision with prior failed row having different wallet), per-payment and rolling daily USD cap on admin-triggered payouts enforced in PaymentService (default $500/payment, $2000/day/guild, exceeding goes to manual_hold), admin DM on successful admin_chat payouts over threshold (default $100, differentiated from failure DMs). Fix P2: log audit for secret leakage, document RLS posture at top of sql/payments.sql. Already using Helius so RPC attacks out of scope. Two wallets already split into solana_grants and solana_payouts providers. Think carefully about wallet-swap and idempotency — they are subtle and easy to half-fix. Goal is end-to-end hardening.

(6) CRITICAL: stamp record['amount_usd']=derived_usd_for_record, record['token_price_usd']=derived_price_for_record, AND mirror onto record['request_payload']['amount_usd'] and record['request_payload']['token_price_usd'] \u2014 but ONLY inside the capped+derived branch. NEVER stamp for uncapped providers or is_test=True (that would break existing tests asserting amount_usd is None). (7) If cap_breach is set: log WARN with redacted wallet, set record['status']='manual_hold', record['last_error']=cap_breach. (8) After create_payment_request returns created and if cap_breach and self._on_cap_breach: await self._on_cap_breach(created). This same path also receives Branch A manual_hold rows from T6. (9) Do NOT add cap logic in execute_payment \u2014 scheduler path must stay untouched. (10) BEFORE writing the code, re-grep amount_usd in tests/test_admin_payments.py and tests/test_social_publish_service.py to confirm every existing amount_usd-is-None assertion uses 'solana' or 'solana_native' (outside default capped_providers); record grep results in executor_notes.",

      "id": "T6",
      "description": "Idempotency collision detection with index-aware branching in PaymentService.request_payment (src/features/payments/payment_service.py:54-61 and 167-174). (1) Confirm get_payment_requests_by_producer at db_handler.py:1853 returns all statuses; add an inline comment documenting this invariant. (2) Rework the early-return: query all_prior_rows, normalize incoming wallet string, define INDEX_EXCLUDED={'failed','cancelled'} and TERMINAL_FOR_RETURN={'confirmed','failed','manual_hold','cancelled'}. Iterate once to find collision_row (different wallet) and blocking_prior_row (any non-index-excluded row). The idempotent early-return must ONLY fire for an active, same-wallet prior row \u2014 terminal rows MUST NOT short-circuit (add inline comment explaining why). (3) Branch A \u2014 slot reuse after terminal failure (blocking_prior_row is None): build record normally but set record['status']='manual_hold' and record['last_error']='idempotency collision: prior wallet differs', call create_payment_request, then await on_cap_breach(created). (4) Branch B \u2014 prior row still holds index slot (blocking_prior_row is not None): log WARN, await on_cap_breach(blocking_prior_row), return None without attempting any insert. (5) Fallback path at 167-174 (post-insert duplicate re-read): apply the same wallet-mismatch check against the re-read row; mismatch \u2192 log WARN, fire on_cap_breach, return None; match \u2192 return canonical row.",
      "depends_on": [],
      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Reworked `PaymentService.request_payment` to be index-aware. Added the `get_payment_requests_by_producer` invariant comment that all statuses are returned. `request_payment` now defines `INDEX_EXCLUDED={'failed','cancelled'}` and `TERMINAL_FOR_RETURN={'confirmed','failed','manual_hold','cancelled'}`, scans prior rows once, blocks different-wallet collisions when any non-index-excluded prior row exists (Branch B: WARN + `on_cap_breach` payload is the prior row + return `None` without insert), only returns an idempotent row when it is active and same-wallet, creates a fresh `manual_hold` row with `last_error='idempotency collision: prior wallet differs'` when only failed/cancelled rows exist (Branch A), and applies the same wallet-mismatch check in the post-insert duplicate reread path. If price lookup raises or returns None/0, cap_usd stays None \u2192 fail-closed to manual_hold with last_error='cap check unavailable: token price missing'. (5) Compute cap_breach: per-payment cap exceeded, OR daily cap exceeded (rolling = db_handler.get_rolling_24h_payout_usd(guild_id, provider_key); rolling + cap_usd > daily_cap). (6) CRITICAL: stamp record['amount_usd']=derived_usd_for_record, record['token_price_usd']=derived_price_for_record, AND mirror onto record['request_payload']['amount_usd'] and record['request_payload']['token_price_usd'] \u2014 but ONLY inside the capped+derived branch. (7) If cap_breach is set: log WARN with redacted wallet, set record['status']='manual_hold', record['last_error']=cap_breach.         Idea:
Harden the Solana payment subsystem against the attack vectors found by the adversarial security review. Fix P0: git history secret audit, prompt-injection hardening in admin_chat (remove LLM-sourced wallet addresses from initiate_payment tool; wallets must come from wallet_registry keyed by mentioned Discord user), null recipient_discord_id confirmation bypass (fail-closed). Fix P1: wallet swap window between test and final payment (verify execute_payment uses stored recipient_wallet string; reject or warn on upsert_wallet during active intents), idempotency slot reuse after forced failure (warn/DM on producer_ref collision with prior failed row having different wallet), per-payment and rolling daily USD cap on admin-triggered payouts enforced in PaymentService (default $500/payment, $2000/day/guild, exceeding goes to manual_hold), admin DM on successful admin_chat payouts over threshold (default $100, differentiated from failure DMs). Fix P2: log audit for secret leakage, document RLS posture at top of sql/payments.sql. Already using Helius so RPC attacks out of scope. Two wallets already split into solana_grants and solana_payouts providers. Think carefully about wallet-swap and idempotency — they are subtle and easy to half-fix. Goal is end-to-end hardening.

    "id": "T6",
    "description": "Idempotency collision detection with index-aware branching in PaymentService.request_payment (src/features/payments/payment_service.py:54-61 and 167-174). (1) Confirm get_payment_requests_by_producer at db_handler.py:1853 returns all statuses; add an inline comment documenting this invariant. (2) Rework the early-return: query all_prior_rows, normalize incoming wallet string, define INDEX_EXCLUDED={'failed','cancelled'} and TERMINAL_FOR_RETURN={'confirmed','failed','manual_hold','cancelled'}. Iterate once to find collision_row (different wallet) and blocking_prior_row (any non-index-excluded row). The idempotent early-return must ONLY fire for an active, same-wallet prior row \u2014 terminal rows MUST NOT short-circuit (add inline comment explaining why). (3) Branch A \u2014 slot reuse after terminal failure (blocking_prior_row is None): build record normally but set record['status']='manual_hold' and record['last_error']='idempotency collision: prior wallet differs', call create_payment_request, then await on_cap_breach(created). (4) Branch B \u2014 prior row still holds index slot (blocking_prior_row is not None): log WARN, await on_cap_breach(blocking_prior_row), return None without attempting any insert. (5) Fallback path at 167-174 (post-insert duplicate re-read): apply the same wallet-mismatch check against the re-read row; mismatch \u2192 log WARN, fire on_cap_breach, return None; match \u2192 return canonical row.",
    "depends_on": [],
    "status": "done",
    "executor_notes": "Reworked `PaymentService.request_payment` to be index-aware.
